se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-1fe96a5d-f640-41b2-bbe1-13f89066bbec" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare (NYSE: NET)의 3분기 FY25 실적 발표 후 +14% 주가 갭 상승</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9e72aa14-60a0-49c3-a947-99d5b19a16e7" style="color:#444746;">1</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9e3bad2a-13eb-4727-993c-ae26fb0bdc6f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">은 단순한 'Beat and Raise'의 결과가 아닙니다. 이는 시장이 Cloudflare를 전통적인 CDN(콘텐츠 전송 네트워크) 및 웹 보안 공급업체에서 차세대 AI 인프라 및 엣지 컴퓨팅 플랫폼으로 재평가하기 시작했음을 시사하는 강력한 신호입니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-08b69229-772f-4ea6-9672-1d48fcadafa7" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-57f14df6-45d6-447c-88e6-6ba91a533575" style="color:#1b1c1d;">핵심 투자 논지는 Cloudflare가 자사의 거대한 글로벌 엣지 네트워크</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-be9ddc67-f0ad-4393-bf8c-a2c81c68851b" style="color:#444746;">2</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0aad6bc1-16d5-4d17-8ea0-7c91b046f21e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">를 활용하여, 하이퍼스케일러(AWS, GCP, Azure)의 '데이터 독점'[User Query]과 SASE(Secure Access Service Edge) 경쟁사들의 파편화된 아키텍처라는 두 가지 핵심 문제를 동시에 해결하는 중립적인 '연결성의 클라우드'(Connectivity Cloud)</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-042c9c6f-43eb-466c-bcf1-ddfd806392e5" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c6958b2c-afc7-4c09-b168-8bb47d827999" style="color:#1b1c1d;">로 자리매김하고 있다는 것입니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0605cc8f-4342-4dd2-8098-54cc644c58b6" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1be91ff9-25f0-45d2-939b-51abfb67691b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">3분기 실적에서 나타난 43% YoY의 폭발적인 잔여수주액(RPO) 성장</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-69b40b1f-46c1-47a1-9640-e30cc8060225" style="color:#444746;">6</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-481a83ca-dd75-424f-a726-04e20662b37c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">은 이 거대한 전략적 전환이 단순한 내러티브가 아닌, 이미 대형 엔터프라이즈 고객들의 실제 계약</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c6905a5b-414b-4639-9ab7-4186f2084759" style="color:#444746;">6</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d0d79f80-1a3c-4b07-8878-8fefa865e5ca" style="color:#1b1c1d;">으로 증명되고 있음을 보여줍니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e7ce693c-dd61-47ed-b93d-feaba48e699d" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e4a6c34e-eb92-4040-9605-eb79709861ff" style="color:#1b1c1d;">본 보고서는 Cloudflare의 재무 성과, 전방 산업, GTM(Go-to-Market) 전략, 그리고 독점적인 기술 해자(Moat)를 심층 분석하여, 동사가 어떻게 AI 및 멀티 클라우드 시대의 '스위스(Switzerland)'가 되어가고 있는지, 그리고 2028년 50억 달러 매출 목표</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1ba41645-b779-4f3a-9584-138a6fc647ef" style="color:#444746;">8</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-31b05bd1-11be-4f53-a6d7-bd764fba7be7" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 달성 가능성을 평가합니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-20b757b9-b848-40d2-8f95-8ea5f3152606" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-afa2d28c-3860-4d86-bda9-5630043fa7b8" style="color:#1b1c1d;">핵심 재무 하이라이트 (Q3 FY25):</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p style="color:#444746;">10</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e5b5b87c-e98d-4f74-bf2e-9aa43827f219" style=""><span Q3 FY25 재무 성과 심층 분석: 숫자로 증명된 성장 모멘텀</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-19d96fd3-b0a6-48b5-923d-77678d5ccf4e" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-4bb8dfb0-a8d5-4811-b40a-871dfb3d459a" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b3d5cf47-553d-47be-97d6-88d8ec6975dd" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-41d27ec0-c041-485c-b240-ea90bb8d7b29" style="color:#1b1c1d;">3분기 실적은 Cloudflare의 성장이 둔화되고 있다는 시장의 우려를 완벽하게 불식시켰습니다. 이는 '두 번째 연속 분기 매출 성장 가속'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c826220e-1e03-461a-b926-1709640b6dc6" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e7307687-4057-4bcf-8fa7-76d46001c6c5" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이라는 점에서 질적으로 매우 우수합니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-7614e5aa-6a5c-4435-afff-18ad4f7da981" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a8fbc2f9-dd04-45cb-8258-31621f39942f" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ed5925cb-cdef-48ab-b4ba-70c98f0d3c60" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-51a498cd-2c2f-4739-a567-2c61bbd3c044" style="color:#1b1c1d;">A. 성장의 핵심 동력, RPO (잔여수주액)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b2a44593-593b-475f-b09d-a9dfc8112e4e" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-276796c5-8425-4e78-aabf-dcd4101c9436" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3911b767-20bb-40c2-a313-eee3ed13aa2c" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f6ca0c04-48be-4ebd-88bb-ba0ec643a9b6" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이번 실적의 하이라이트는 단연 43% YoY 성장을 기록한 잔여수주액(RPO)입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6a487622-8970-4954-95c6-d63d55c6cacb" style="color:#444746;">6</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b31a5c00-b928-4abb-84ac-86e9a1aae2b1" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-41552260-d7c8-4060-b02f-e73ae0998cab" style="color:#1b1c1d;">RPO의 질적 분석: RPO는 이미 계약되었으나 아직 매출로 인식되지 않은 미래 수익의 선행 지표입니다. 21억 4천만 달러에 달하는 이 수치의 43% 성장은 Cloudflare가 현재 $2B 수준의 연간 매출</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-80ec1484-130f-43ff-a421-628c71e77d39" style="color:#444746;">16</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-eb6b6cdb-f36b-40eb-8ac3-f3cb4345e58b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 이미 넘어서는 규모의 계약을 확보했음을 의미합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a5ffdff9-74b5-4b61-9f5c-1b7c26e39797" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f9e6fe5a-d205-458c-8c56-11d140e0c286" style="color:#1b1c1d;">cRPO의 의미: 이 RPO 중 64%는 12개월 내 매출로 실현(cRPO, Current RPO)될 예정입니다[User Query]. 이는 강력한 단기 매출 가시성을 제공하며, 현재의 가이던스 상향이 매우 보수적일 수 있음을 시사합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d7455304-1935-4ac5-8f2d-c6f94e85af96" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a7c140df-8c90-4d5d-b473-48a038a087cc" style="color:#1b1c1d;">어닝 콜의 단서: 어닝 콜에서 경영진은 이 RPO 가속화의 요인으로 "제품 단위 판매를 넘어 전사 차원 영업을 추구한 결과"이자 "숙련된 영업팀의 규모 증가" 및 "대형 계약(연 $1~5M)"의 성공을 직접적으로 언급했습니다[User Query].</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-809ef204-1967-45ed-9dd1-6c5e30115c27" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0cc0529f-208d-49da-90d9-81d4fb193417" style="color:#1b1c1d;">RPO의 성장이 현재 매출 성장률(31%)을 12%p나 상회(43%)한다는 것은 '수주잔고(Backlog)'가 기록적인 속도로 쌓이고 있음을 의미합니다. 이는 향후 4~6분기 동안 매출 성장이 현재의 31% 수준을 유지하거나 오히려 더 가속화될 수 있음을 시사하는 가장 강력한 선행 지표입니다. 이는 Cloudflare가 성숙 단계에 진입한 것이 아니라, 엔터프라이즈 시장 침투라는 '두 번째 성장 곡선'에 막 진입했음을 보여줍니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-61b40c59-3ecd-4bfa-83fb-0253b9bb6f6a" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-1a102d59-a8eb-45c8-89de-68411babceae" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-816344bb-bd83-4681-8bd5-3cfe4b70f201" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-30f83426-8df4-4557-b397-f6c10926a80e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">B. 수익성 분석: AI 투자와 영업 레버리지의 균형</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ba2eaad3-9f5d-4cc3-9248-faaf4bb9951e" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-8c074bcc-e9b5-4d61-9372-1454eadc11cc" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-631d2956-3c1f-441c-b7c1-40ebbbed9539" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a672b842-20df-429d-9b7c-5dda84252f14" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare는 성장 가속과 동시에 수익성 개선을 입증했습니다.</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d95287cc-9a5d-4b11-9a30-f7a9ffd5bddb" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-35592d0e-c744-4e33-999a-51574f901131" style="color:#1b1c1d;">매출 및 OPM: 매출은 컨센서스($545M)를 $17M 초과 달성($562M)했으며</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9e01205a-05cc-4b4d-a704-ec64622fb441" style="color:#444746;">10</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-eb6b0cd7-37da-436b-8f90-529788b2ac61" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 비-GAAP 영업이익률(OPM)은 15.3%를 기록하며 예상치(13.6%)와 전년 동기(14.8%)를 모두 상회했습니다[User Query].</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c38febde-974a-47d7-97b0-38d4804d0297" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c537ae74-ffdf-4516-9e42-d84b536429b0" style="color:#1b1c1d;">EPS: 이러한 매출 성장과 비용 통제가 결합되어 비-GAAP EPS는 $0.27로, 예상치 $0.23을 17% 이상 크게 상회했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c965f1ec-6ce8-45aa-ab86-5ba5524706be" style="color:#444746;">13</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c2906e5a-2a37-4dcf-8052-31a08625fd3f" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-20a9ede0-dc72-4bbb-8f73-66519f64c864" style="color:#1b1c1d;">총이익률(Gross Margin)의 함정: 주목할 점은 비-GAAP 총이익률이 75.3%로, 전년 동기의 78.8%</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-55fe488c-242a-449f-91b8-6a6dbefbf7d8" style="color:#444746;">18</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-73fd126d-2e51-4afc-835e-9161f76ea2ad" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 대비 하락</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-184e1adc-2899-4896-af01-6640da96359b" style="color:#444746;">13</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b7a6baf1-58da-4a6b-a4f9-883cf60e7d89" style="color:#1b1c1d;">했다는 것입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-205ef278-b26c-4c89-8e5e-f4dfc3858276" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f6e62e49-c156-44bb-9ec7-d468e73c881f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">원인: 이 하락은 어닝 콜 Q&amp;A의 "GPU 용량" 관련 답변[User Query]과 Morningstar의 분석</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-eadffaa1-232b-43ec-99c4-d80f1c320af6" style="color:#444746;">15</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-48c50d44-df5d-4966-a121-ada0a5c61122" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에서 명확히 드러납니다. 이는 AI 인프라(GPU)에 대한 선제적 투자와 네트워크 비용 증가에 따른 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-7d3c2a37-a8ad-446f-862f-37e379bf08aa" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>전략적</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-fb143b6d-8e31-445f-a69a-9b7c41bf4a80" style="color:#1b1c1d;">인 하락입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f791b6d6-9366-4e25-bcd9-754dabda951a" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3ad9e49d-3571-4305-9c4d-13a7e9630e7f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">해석: 총이익률이 하락했음에도 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-96db2e32-bd1a-4615-9e94-65c1d6f824e3" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>영업이익률(OPM)이 개선</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-67ec74d3-2c4f-48a4-839d-9c63751ddb83" style="color:#1b1c1d;">되었다는 것은, R&amp;D와 S&amp;M 등 기타 운영비용(Opex)에서 강력한 레버리지가 발생하고 있음을 의미합니다. 회사는 AI라는 새로운 기회를 잡기 위해 의도적으로 총이익률을 일부 희생하고 있으며, 이는 장기적으로 더 큰 플랫폼 수익으로 돌아올 수 있습니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1f6ece20-bb5f-485b-b851-9323c79a44ff" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7c1fc174-5ea7-4043-8349-9212e8cd28ce" style="color:#1b1c1d;">순매출유지율(NRR)이 119%로 반등</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e249aa53-eb93-4513-9490-b95254c33da2" style="color:#444746;">10</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8c352b5b-c1e4-4ca1-9f87-cdd1cf777293" style="color:#1b1c1d;">한 것은 'Pool of Funds'(PoF) 모델의 성공을 입증하는 핵심 변곡점입니다. PoF 도입 초기에는 기존 계약이 소비 기반으로 전환되면서 NRR이 일시적으로 하락 압력을 받았습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-faacdd56-6793-4136-9d75-c35cd73752c5" style="color:#444746;">19</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2f90b9a9-902d-4b9f-9f06-2b7999198c40" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 하지만 어닝 콜 Q&amp;A에서 언급했듯이[User Query], 이제 고객들이 이 '선불 적립금'을 소진하고 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-b0ad4cdd-37c1-48aa-96e7-02b21af04089" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>새로운 제품(Upselling)을 채택</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1cdbd662-64b6-406a-8190-088d9ac6637a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">하기 시작하면서 NRR이 다시 상승</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c624c3e5-39c8-4dab-a427-57f4323594cf" style="color:#444746;">8</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-67ba1721-0865-42cc-8f52-0f0a567f99d9" style="color:#1b1c1d;">하고 있습니다. 이는 PoF가 NRR의 단기 변동성을 유발하지만, 장기적으로는 더 높은 NRR을 위한 '슬링샷' 역할을 하고 있음을 보여줍니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8fde215e-b181-4855-8b97-0a38ceaf493f" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-7c350cf5-15ef-4e69-8e47-c06867630873" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-42636389-f63d-42fd-a102-89f1203df3c4" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a61633f6-636a-4cd1-9804-fde5b372fb8a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">C. 가이던스 상향 및 장기 목표</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8a5ce6a5-d5a6-47a7-a4c9-0cd7e3392cd2" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a7fc1b20-08da-4181-9a5a-776f91b506f9" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d0f97e43-584f-4488-8c64-61395d295196" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-48e26bdc-cd9b-4c03-914f-d70ba35f7f9d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare는 3분기 호실적을 바탕으로 FY25 연간 가이던스를 상향 조정했습니다.</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-59a494ee-be6b-496d-9a67-a8e9d92881db" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-76e7e86c-cc1d-4b9a-89e2-1429aa25e10c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">FY25 신규 가이던스: 매출 $2.14B (기존 $2.12B), OPM 13.9% (기존 13.5%), EPS $0.91 (기존 $0.86) </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-22993056-6836-47a8-81b0-9fdf99b5878b" style="color:#444746;">1</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e86c5714-0cfb-4743-a610-13604ac7acfc" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9245aa19-a3cb-4af8-b0f7-9182b8ff3754" style="color:#1b1c1d;">장기 목표: 경영진은 2028년 4분기까지 연간 매출 50억 달러 달성 목표를 재확인했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-802d6e3d-dc24-4557-8a03-15ee8932cacf" style="color:#444746;">8</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3514751d-9568-4d55-b6b4-285525d552ad" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 현재 $2.14B의 매출 가이던스에서 $5B 목표를 달성하려면 향후 3년간 약 25-30%의 연평균성장률(CAGR)이 필요합니다. 3분기 RPO가 43% 성장하고 SASE</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-eb726f51-4e5e-4e24-873b-ebb8eb0607e4" style="color:#444746;">21</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f132897b-ddcf-4162-94f6-996a12c418d0" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 및 엣지 AI</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a607f30e-1418-458c-8228-e073dcba9576" style="color:#444746;">22</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-dea350db-d2a0-4893-aadb-f53f44794877" style="color:#1b1c1d;">라는 거대한 시장이 열리고 있음을 감안할 때, 이 목표는 단순한 포부가 아니라 달성 가능한 현실적인 수치로 판단됩니다.</span></p></li></ul><!-- } SE-TEXT -->
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div> <div class="se-component se-horizontalLine se-l-line1" id="SE-11cfb989-8a13-4b9f-b01e-a64d36ff8a44">

<div class="se-module se-module-text">

</div> <div class="se-component se-table se-l-default" id="SE-cf562f80-a8de-4ec8-87d5-4a2d0f212df4">
<div class="se-component-content">
<div class="se-section se-section-table se-l-default se-section-align-left" style="width: 100.0%;">
<div class="se-table-container">
<table class="se-table-content" style="border:none;border-collapse:collapse;">
<tbody><tr class="se-tr"><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 16.66%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-54a50c6f-7a2d-4266-9627-88243e1521a6" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-37efaefb-b8fa-4615-a194-61f748d00b95" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">지표</span></p></div>
</td><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 16.66%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">

</div> <div class="se-component se-text se-l-default" id="SE-c1e6c9e7-d983-41b3-9cf0-dbf3324dcf10">
<div class="se-component-content">
<div class="se-section se-section-text se-l-default">
<div class="se-module se-module-text">
<!-- SE-TEXT { --><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d0e207a7-9d99-4ad9-b19b-e0a1ecaaa8ee" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8ed3ec0a-192f-45d3-b14c-037f004666f5" style="color:#1b1c1d;">III. GTM 전략 및 고객 분석: 엔터프라이즈 시장 장악 (사용자 질의: "고객이 어디인지도")</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f0a1007f-2f84-4651-b5bf-80cac5e1821d" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-0789ba65-9ac4-427c-9d9e-55cbe0e017d7" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-4ef4fb6d-721b-43ae-ba65-e837096b47c7" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e9f56e25-fc3b-4972-9b2b-3abac0c80254" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare의 성장은 더 이상 개발자 중심의 제품 주도 성장(PLG)에만 의존하지 않습니다. 3분기 RPO의 폭발적 성장은 엔터프라이즈 중심의 'Top-Down' 영업으로의 성공적인 전환</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6ed50079-b01b-45ce-9884-518e617ba9c1" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c51e80ff-cc21-435d-b658-4ea6586d3bc2" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 증명합니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-6f5f8ac6-22f7-4a1d-9771-a3821c93d496" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-1c3e6776-9ff1-436b-962b-e4a1bbf1392e" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-31154d98-a988-44f5-b3c0-959807a96e96" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-eb7b0491-b47c-4f7d-90d1-5fc1ffad4d6d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">A. GTM 전략의 진화: PLG에서 엔터프라이즈 세일즈로</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-012e08c9-5162-4518-b7a1-7c6430451d45" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-52c5c01b-52e5-4ab5-ab16-94819f341837" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b279871f-5123-4f8b-9ec8-ee108d52e26b" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-20936a37-b835-4a3c-9c9f-a658adf9f35c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">과거 (PLG): 개발자가 무료 플랜</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7313572c-197d-4257-b753-b52e4a858300" style="color:#444746;">23</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-75a49828-c876-4fdf-ad0f-c70ee8aa4f88" style="color:#1b1c1d;">으로 가입하고, 필요에 따라 유료 플랜으로 전환하는 'Bottom-up' 방식이었습니다. 이는 100만 명 이상의 개발자 생태계</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-89f958c2-0f1a-4308-8a01-4c451286c3f3" style="color:#444746;">24</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c70f627c-f4e2-47bd-b297-8ad7108b01b3" style="color:#1b1c1d;">를 구축하는 데 기여했습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-013b6f31-1185-498b-8872-3549e0882384" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-812e0ff5-cdb3-416e-be36-81d27ce9146f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">현재 (PLG + Enterprise Sales): 어닝 콜에서 언급된 "숙련된 영업팀의 규모 증가"와 "전사 차원 영업"[User Query]이 핵심입니다. Cloudflare는 이제 대형 기업의 C-Level을 공략하는 강력한 영업 조직</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-077ba89f-87c0-48cf-941b-6b471f083dc2" style="color:#444746;">25</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-614a3429-750c-4bfd-9339-bbc7a5ffec3b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 운영하며, PLG는 잠재 고객을 발굴하는 'Trojan horse'(트로이 목마)</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-769b6539-0156-4971-bf92-3ca8b81e8448" style="color:#444746;">26</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a56aacf8-7eb4-468d-bc2a-7bbc05618d5d" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 역할을 합니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a7008d1a-0676-4ff8-ba23-4fd42ed6943d" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-efb411b8-c051-4bb7-9803-907bd7927182" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-eaabdec9-a399-4b77-b15a-7dc439ee24d1" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9758604a-91ed-47ae-a3e4-3ac8778e85eb" style="color:#1b1c1d;">B. 핵심 영업 모델: 'Pool of Funds' (PoF)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e56f9005-1812-4770-bf3a-fa289a2e5ac6" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a1892608-1478-4f8b-9ef8-b7b829832336" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d162d6bf-c50c-4989-97c0-a3413a7bfce6" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9aa910c5-e584-4ad0-9eda-5dae62b78f6d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">PoF는 Cloudflare의 엔터프라이즈 전략의 핵심 무기입니다.</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-73eabec6-a50c-44f1-b4c6-217c7185d50c" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f5917c39-64ef-4486-913b-799596d3a6a5" style="color:#1b1c1d;">작동 방식: '선불 적립'[User Query] 모델로, 고객이 2~3년</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-52ccbf24-d516-4a5f-ac82-594edd86ca9c" style="color:#444746;">6</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-555bb11c-30ed-4506-8fd8-46350a47f47d" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 동안 사용할 금액(예: $5M)을 선불로 약정합니다. 고객사 내부의 모든 부서는 이 'Pool'에서 자금을 차감하며 Cloudflare의 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-c326ae9c-638a-4434-8f58-8c5ef0ca91ca" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>모든</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ae084878-b543-4bcb-94a5-816dd5211641" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 제품</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-11f69e09-9075-4bd8-b656-f6ce94275f72" style="color:#444746;">27</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-35f5b507-2a26-46f9-9a44-0f96bf5754a5" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 별도의 구매 절차 없이 즉시 사용할 수 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1fe9b56d-96e0-4708-b70b-383d5d73c9ea" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-bba744ca-e5f9-4288-9d1b-683249d50897" style="color:#1b1c1d;">어닝 콜 분석: PoF는 총 계약액(TCV)의 '낮은 두 자릿수(10% 초반)' 비중을 차지하며[User Query], 이는 PoF가 아직 초기 단계임에도 불구하고 의미 있는 규모로 성장했음</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b5332400-4fb5-4706-9ad8-5314855b89c0" style="color:#444746;">28</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2bc64d39-0b9a-4ad8-9d58-1c91a2fa6e53" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 보여줍니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-daa1b278-559f-4800-ae61-a539fa2d1a2a" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b2dd0d22-7523-4e6d-b2e1-ee0be1e7d20f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">효과:</span></p></li></ul><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-2a8288b9-0b2d-4a7b-bcca-85f26e9bf0b1" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7f61ac1c-ff26-44af-8f08-dc05531f597b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">조달 마찰 감소: 제품을 하나씩 구매할 필요 없이 플랫폼 전체에 접근</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8ce0ca5d-e76f-458f-9fc4-f57ea9314ff3" style="color:#444746;">29</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2d575c20-e8de-47f1-953a-2f2a7ec017df" style="color:#1b1c1d;">할 수 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-10ca1228-c9f6-483e-9058-560c469d833a" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9b7a7238-d39a-42ba-8a51-69d0b6f5c3a8" style="color:#1b1c1d;">강력한 업셀링: 어닝 콜에서 언급했듯이 "고객의 업셀링 이끌기 좋고 NRR 상방요인"으로 작용합니다[User Query].</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-853a6b1a-7b30-4545-bb6f-b1289c797836" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d3b7e821-3d5a-406a-8616-3822273979a2" style="color:#1b1c1d;">장기 계약: 3~5년의 장기 계약</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-97955ec1-e1e9-48ee-8045-77fd0a4dd006" style="color:#444746;">6</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3a12e8bf-9432-4b90-86fe-50eb97c1db08" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 유도하여 RPO를 급증시킵니다.</span></p></li></ol><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-fbe87f56-814b-4721-8f69-df4b1c332b7d" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-b53fc627-828b-445f-895e-20de52f6f25b" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a56dd908-989f-460d-92d0-28df5dc28487" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7b2f3498-d1f8-4907-b102-f45aa4c54e8d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">C. 고객 분석: 대형 고객(Large Customers)이 성장을 주도</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e050026e-30b2-4917-8006-ead28c3135e7" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-61790b7d-833a-4244-83fe-792df6a38595" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9351a753-e58b-4583-9bc8-d2f19b9cb783" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4d98bf35-4a39-4fc1-a6a1-c6aee2216947" style="color:#1b1c1d;">고객 구성: Cloudflare는 수백만 개의 무료 고객</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2987726a-d3ea-432f-a843-169f7d54cd04" style="color:#444746;">30</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ab2948ce-6f44-4f19-8e71-11b1be00c01d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">과 295,552개의 유료 고객</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a2c18ea4-8cd6-438a-8e29-13a9a7b6e167" style="color:#444746;">13</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-226aec77-910d-4653-a3c6-5211813550ff" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 보유하고 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c166f278-757a-4380-923b-21ea86e8e73b" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ef62420a-c1fb-4872-900b-c93029bd4c1a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">핵심은 대형 고객: 성장의 질은 연간 $100K 이상을 지출하는 대형 고객에 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f78bc1f8-586b-4c70-a05c-9db5558152da" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7ce2e91f-4dc7-4e25-9f03-2c1edd0b9c87" style="color:#1b1c1d;">$100K+ 고객: 4,009개로 전년 동기 대비 23% 증가했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e78d35c0-171d-4ca7-b275-dde0701e2657" style="color:#444746;">10</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9eb0df06-0860-4ccf-adfd-9f587ae1eaff" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a57adc03-7d8f-469d-8c06-e130272b846f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">매출 기여도: 이 4,009개의 고객(전체 유료 고객의 약 1.4%)이 **전체 매출의 73%**를 기여합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-34339bb7-90ce-4bc4-adbc-58a3feecd7fa" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3f111f67-deeb-41ee-bb3f-e260046bd682" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이는 전년 동기의 67%</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5a787eff-f381-44b0-963e-de414757ae14" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ad9d3650-9841-4518-a93a-131d3a972f36" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에서 크게 증가한 수치로, 대형 고객으로의 매출 집중도가 높아지고 있음을 의미합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-bead4d3b-b695-4cb3-ad78-a9d5aa3a7575" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a785e6af-b713-4be4-8526-53ee0480fdc4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">초대형 고객 모멘텀: $1M 및 $5M 이상 지출 고객</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b67c4ca8-8eaa-4eac-afdd-23dc6384541b" style="color:#444746;">6</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-dc0f3c3b-aa0d-454d-8096-19560ca47f8a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에서 기록적인 성장을 보였으며, 이는 SASE 및 플랫폼 전반의 대형 계약</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-78aa68f4-21ec-4fa0-ab3f-bd9d2637ed1a" style="color:#444746;">1</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c5ddc106-4ea7-4fbc-add3-c9455c06e784" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에서 비롯됩니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e6ac30b8-08bf-48d3-8127-cafc3f85c004" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-bee5d707-e3f1-4ffa-a5d8-abeb6c10d69b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">주요 계약 사례:</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ae5401cb-5210-43e4-9073-242928f40497" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-77df2423-e95f-4949-89e8-1c1594a7d075" style="color:#1b1c1d;">$20M Fortune 100 기술 기업: 5년 만기 PoF 계약으로, Cloudflare 역사상 가장 큰 '신규' 고객 확보 사례입니다. Application Security와 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-57369bc0-6d88-4f11-a65b-cd23bb604aea" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>Workers</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3d9f8b2b-97c3-482f-b501-dce0e836feff" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 플랫폼을 모두 포함합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-bb91e491-5229-4302-999e-20326f54fe80" style="color:#444746;">27</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-47c6e20a-570e-43c9-aa5f-720438e71208" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ca27c2bb-d1ce-41b3-8606-52537292b4a5" style="color:#1b1c1d;">$20M 미국 내각 기관: 2년 계약으로, 12개 이상의 레거시 솔루션을 대체하며 FedRAMP 포트폴리오를 표준화했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-dc1638b9-7e60-4ccf-b193-f8ea84b46eef" style="color:#444746;">6</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-370aa8f7-c73a-4fb2-973b-9ba18117b566" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-900d1ee8-6a64-4daa-a556-1cb137fdc864" style="color:#1b1c1d;">$34.3M 유럽 기술 기업: 5년 SASE 계약.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1c545772-1221-4b02-bc52-86e3c05a6d7c" style="color:#444746;">1</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e31c9e08-8b3c-4dbe-96e8-fb9837ca3566" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-83422d7d-3882-4a7f-bdf4-9c6710b79e88" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare의 고객 기반은 '바벨(Barbell)' 전략을 구사합니다. 한쪽 끝에는 거대한 수의 무료/개발자 고객</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-555b8adb-7c27-4909-894f-26ee7be7767e" style="color:#444746;">24</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-49b8ff7e-671d-423b-9576-b370619ae6e5" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이 네트워크 효과(데이터 수집, 위협 인텔리전스)와 미래의 유료 고객 풀을 제공합니다. 다른 쪽 끝에는 전체 매출의 73%를 차지하는 4,000여 개의 핵심 엔터프라이즈 고객</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a5e89d2e-d139-4b30-9b76-0719ba1ff542" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-253524a0-51df-4629-83de-dcf43b6bfc8d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이 있습니다. PoF 모델은 이 두 고객층을 연결하는 다리입니다. 즉, 엔터프라이즈가 PoF 계약을 체결하면, 해당 기업의 개발자(PLG 고객층)가 마찰 없이 Workers AI와 R2를 사용하게 되는 구조입니다.</span></p><!-- } SE-TEXT -->
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div> <div class="se-component se-text se-l-default" id="SE-d9316330-866e-4ccd-8983-b06859b3d425">
<div class="se-component-content">

Act 1: CDN (콘텐츠 전송 네트워크)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-59c68d84-37e4-4f53-892a-fc00b7c08bbf" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi 플레이어입니다. 경쟁사(Zscaler, PANW)가 여러 제품을 인수/통합한 반면, Cloudflare는 단일 플랫폼/네트워크에서 모든 SASE 기능을 제공</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" style="color:#1b1c1d;">한다는 아키텍처의 우월성을 가집니다. $34.3M 규모의 SASE 계약</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-959f8d9f-9ca7-4415-9a93-a10080216e49" 플레이어임을 증명합니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-67c3c1b3-8c2b-4568-b8c4-1033fafd3401" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-07eafb27-6640-4675-9a71-415c5231988e" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ff0f990d-0c71-4c0a-8e40-f5b915d66e16" style="color:#1b1c1d;">시장 현황: AI, IoT, 5G의 확산</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9280420b-412f-45a8-9fd1-f7dc03867c03" style="color:#444746;">49</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-727f9463-bcab-4efc-b2ab-eb12d37bf943" style="color:#1b1c1d;">으로 데이터가 생성되는 '엣지'에서 즉각적인 처리가 요구되고 있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f0bafd82-e30f-41b3-a794-02fd0fb1b3e0" style="color:#444746;">50</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e7f20215-e66d-4a9d-832f-187179f47977" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 2025년까지 대기업의 40% 이상이 엣지 컴퓨팅을 도입할 것</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6a603ac9-6178-428b-9f30-c1276292a610" style="color:#444746;">22</span><span class="se-fs-fs15
