class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-efcae707-3ad0-4312-8209-13decc4631e3" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-fe378534-de0a-4b6b-9531-27a3095f09c0" style="color:#1b1c1d;">핵심 트렌드: AI '학습'은 중앙 집중식 데이터센터에서 이루어지지만, AI '추론'(Inference)은 사용자에게 밀접한 엣지에서</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5db08afa-7112-4251-befd-680d12674099" style="color:#444746;">51</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8f37fca7-2834-4459-bc8d-70d70d5da184" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 저지연(low-latency)으로 실행되어야 합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-53f7581d-c4cc-46ba-a37e-4a3c59c676ab" style="color:#444746;">22</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a5c48123-81c6-4f84-bd4f-d3ab1fa151e4" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d14e0669-3ac7-422a-bf9f-baac74f1dd5d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare의 지위: 이는 Cloudflare의 최종 승부처입니다. Cloudflare의 330개 이상 도시에 분산된 엣지 네트워크</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9cc8f6f7-e562-4b76-ad7d-3ca86d749eda" style="color:#444746;">2</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-73851f62-1768-4eab-b486-a04834c1fd85" style="color:#1b1c1d;">는 AI 추론을 실행하기에 가장 이상적인 인프라입니다. 'Workers AI'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a52aaad7-0345-4581-b44d-8d4af5c4b87d" style="color:#444746;">52</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-884f2c0d-fb77-407c-9460-da1826e3f493" style="color:#1b1c1d;">는 개발자들이 이 네트워크에서 직접 AI 모델을 실행할 수 있게 하는 플랫폼입니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5e1e1832-e3e5-4005-8be1-b5ad904088d7" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-100d8143-0703-47da-af12-8e40932d1f69" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare의 전략은 이 세 시장을 통합하는 것입니다. 1) (Act 1) CDN으로 글로벌 네트워크를 구축했습니다. 2) (Act 2) 이 네트워크에 SASE 보안 스택을 올려 엔터프라이즈의 트래픽을 확보했습니다. 3) (Act 3) 이제 이 트래픽이 흐르는 네트워크를 개발자에게 개방하여 (Workers AI), CDN 콘텐츠와 SASE 보안 정책이 적용된 상태에서 AI 추론을 실행하게 합니다. 이는 경쟁사가 모방할 수 없는 강력한 시너지입니다. 예를 들어, Zscaler는 엣지 컴퓨팅 플랫폼이 없고, AWS는 네트워크 중립적인 SASE 솔루션이 약합니다. Cloudflare는 이 모든 것을 '단일 플랫폼'에서 제공합니다.</span></p><!-- } SE-TEXT -->
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div> <div class="se-component se-horizontalLine se-l-line1" id="SE-c28063ff-cd6c-4ce5-a1e0-e07e086c2b1a">
<div class="se-component-content">
<div class="se-section se-section-horizontalLine se-l-line1 se-section-align-">
<div class="se-module se-module-horizontalLine">
<hr class="se-hr"/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div> <div class="se-component se-text se-l-default" id="SE-1aab03e4-d43c-4fe6-b469-76582246d364">
<div class="se-component-content">
<div class="se-section se-section-text se-l-default">
<div class="se-module se-module-text">
<!-- SE-TEXT { --><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b3ef7caf-01da-4f80-858a-280389270832" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-607f312a-4625-452b-9394-e87bfeb46ce4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">V. 기술 전략 분석: AI 추론 시장을 겨냥한 "Edge" 해자(Moat)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-dd1bd2c5-0e13-47f2-aedd-1900675a4514" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-69051da1-8625-47b8-9db6-76421219e9a9" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-fce61e16-88b5-4386-b7f7-28a1187a60fc" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5e1c9875-7c07-4ab4-a6c1-abd9f71ff0ac" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare의 AI 전략은 하이퍼스케일러와 정면으로 경쟁하는 것이 아니라, 그들이 취약한 '엣지 추론' 시장을 선점하는 것입니다. 이는 기술적 차별성과 파괴적인 비즈니스 모델(Egress Fee 제로)에 기반합니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-48138cad-b0a0-47b9-a5a2-54c4e55330d3" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-f2fe01ad-f398-4eff-b603-3b66c298a7ed" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e7fed305-57b5-40a7-8154-df76a93c68e0" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2251cf4a-53d7-478e-9e88-802fb6605eec" style="color:#1b1c1d;">A. AI 시장의 'On-Ramp' 전략: AI Gateway</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f0768f99-b828-41b2-be6e-6cf0af581a81" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-ae3b908e-f41f-4297-9093-a68ebf7a552b" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-03cd1f25-205a-4b01-9d15-26421685a270" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b8076eda-ba48-423e-ba01-10f2f55fae69" style="color:#1b1c1d;">어닝 콜 Q&amp;A에서 "AI추론 매출은 미미"하지만 "SW제품"이 초기 채택을 이끈다고 언급되었습니다[User Query]. 이 'SW제품'은 AI Gateway입니다.</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-dc02d653-7e28-4f57-abd6-7c8adebd0f7f" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-75e2f397-3a92-41da-ab95-7d454fa21203" style="color:#1b1c1d;">AI Gateway란? 기업이 OpenAI, Anthropic, Workers AI 등</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-766de48e-bf01-40c9-881d-8bc8859b3c4b" style="color:#444746;">53</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b6199548-e669-464c-ad0a-184a1e6efa65" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 다양한 AI 모델을 사용할 때, 그 사이에 위치하는 '관문'입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c6d39b93-595d-4bf7-a25e-30251170b37f" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2ffd8061-deb7-4d35-9b17-c67c8bc8c1d4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">핵심 기능 (비용 절감):</span></p></li></ul><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3bf0591d-b8ca-4063-82dc-9252a3908cd4" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6affd75a-7360-466f-a6d4-a27b501dba92" style="color:#1b1c1d;">캐싱(Caching): 동일한 쿼리(정보요청)가 반복될 경우, AI 모델을 다시 호출하는 대신 캐시된 응답을 제공하여 "불필요한 쿼리로 발생하는 높은 비용을 상쇄"합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4e83ee89-6406-4cb3-b6cf-1b3204ed3946" style="color:#444746;">54</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-cce96d21-e7e1-43bb-8901-23a6fba9415e" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-cf86beef-f8d0-4185-bd00-bf09403eb43a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">속도 제한(Rate Limiting): 갑작스러운 쿼리 폭주로 인한 비용 급증을 제어합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b862dd48-fc45-49e8-82cd-8d29118a10c5" style="color:#444746;">53</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b93600ec-8ffd-491a-9f1f-f3b11d46a56b" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a7388c6e-7c66-436e-a4df-a3640635f9b4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">관찰 가능성(Observability): 모든 AI 요청을 한 곳에서 모니터링하고 비용을 추적합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4a916fa3-e29d-49a0-973f-1a1bc7d5fd27" style="color:#444746;">56</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-87943c4a-47fa-4069-b515-a51f2e753eea" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5a258d96-bbae-4c16-836e-cbe48e70c3df" style="color:#1b1c1d;">전략적 의미 (Land-and-Expand): 기업은 AI Gateway를 도입하는 즉시 AI 비용 절감 효과를 봅니다. Cloudflare는 이 Gateway를 통해 해당 기업의 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-d6185dca-1237-4fc1-a52f-d2244393ee1d" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>모든 AI 워크로드</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8795afd0-80d7-4f9a-8ee7-f040961ed4da" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에 대한 가시성을 확보합니다. 이는 Cloudflare가 자사의 'Workers AI'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3256322e-a6ef-487a-aa66-5f7011c947ec" style="color:#444746;">52</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0c0eda49-04a9-4d5e-a0fa-749ad561acc1" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 플랫폼을 업셀링할 수 있는 완벽한 진입로(On-Ramp)가 됩니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9ee07508-cfc8-4938-a24c-4a495411ae5b" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-2ab672e8-33c7-4d9d-aa04-b713a45ab185" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-87b855f5-75b1-4842-beb1-e2bbaa011b04" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c1eec5b5-e32a-47af-b96c-1cfc66eaec14" style="color:#1b1c1d;">B. 기술 차별점 1: 고효율 GPU 스케줄링 (Omni &amp; Infire)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3e4be6b1-21dd-495b-9a14-ef5e8a828609" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-17e28a68-a73b-4a0c-a790-f26680bf1c79" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-87d30d4b-4658-421c-8d75-c429a2f9201d" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f87fc2a8-ad26-4999-9890-c3fd0dcebdcf" style="color:#1b1c1d;">어닝 콜 Q&amp;A에서 경영진은 GPU 용량이 "지금은 용량 충분"하며, 그 이유로 '스케줄링 기술'과 '멀티 테넌트' 아키텍처를 꼽았습니다[User Query].</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-85efa5f3-e17b-4523-ae8a-0514ec327a1e" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a3f584de-ce95-4864-9ddf-1604abdeccf9" style="color:#1b1c1d;">하이퍼스케일러의 문제: 하이퍼스케일러는 고객에게 GPU가 탑재된 전체 서버(VM)를 임대[User Query]합니다. 만약 고객이 이 GPU를 10%만 사용해도, 나머지 90%는 유휴 상태가 되어 비효율적입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-4a096045-a70e-4056-9df8-b0d24f085ffc" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-32098262-b7ce-410f-afe0-97a149813c63" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare의 해결책:</span></p></li></ul><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-6a37f185-4932-49e8-9d19-c4c33c09eb09" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f355e779-8cb7-4ca6-80fb-5c761b17261f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">'Omni' 플랫폼 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3699bb24-d363-4871-8baf-2ddd165a6df2" style="color:#444746;">57</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8b1ea2a9-9efa-48bf-a25c-bb9314fde098" style="color:#1b1c1d;">: Cloudflare가 자체 개발한 이 플랫폼은 '경량 격리'(lightweight isolation) 및 '메모리 오버커밋'(memory over-commitment) 기술을 사용합니다. 이는 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-d996bc7b-f1bb-48c9-9b41-4af53d8d6574" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>단일 GPU</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b0de3a15-73a3-4aa3-87ec-795964c68f27" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에서 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-d8898b72-e632-47b7-86dd-0a80363a4df0" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>여러 개의 AI 모델</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5562a9dd-54f0-4cff-90a8-dbc6e069fc8d" style="color:#444746;">57</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0a045c12-59d7-4f1b-b348-ac7f8cae95ef" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 동시에 실행할 수 있게 합니다. 즉, A 고객의 Llama 모델과 B 고객의 Mistral 모델이 물리적으로 동일한 GPU를 안전하게 공유합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0b557d67-0017-4b75-9924-f3940fe9ae06" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0b1cfca2-7814-4acf-91fa-77b2cfe7acc1" style="color:#1b1c1d;">'Infire' 엔진 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e1000d2f-b06a-4cdb-8555-de4512aee6e5" style="color:#444746;">58</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-fcbbc801-1610-49f0-9be5-ba646887c98d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">: Rust로 작성된 고효율 추론 엔진으로, '연속적 배치'(continuous batching) 및 '페이지형 KV 캐시'(Paged KV Cache) 기술</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1f7a3a80-29e2-448b-a9aa-cae0d7f9a507" style="color:#444746;">58</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c1276982-81e4-4860-8a8c-05e192bb821b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 통해 여러 요청을 병렬로 처리하여 GPU의 유휴 시간을 최소화합니다.</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8a0c8f13-1c16-4dca-9d8a-835f7fc9437d" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-eec66976-f456-4717-a00f-5cd97f2f82de" style="color:#1b1c1d;">결과: 이 기술들 덕분에 Cloudflare는 70-80%의 GPU 가동률을 달성[User Query]합니다. 이는 하이퍼스케일러의 일반적인 가동률보다 훨씬 높은 수치입니다. Cloudflare의 해자는 GPU의 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-925c7567-9ce3-4ad6-9051-c7d85bdd111e" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>수량</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-fe827073-e955-4b61-ae02-1bf03549c8ed" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이 아니라, GPU를 마지막 한 방울까지 활용하는 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-6d1ad680-c576-44ac-9ab4-a462422a37c2" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>소프트웨어 기술</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-cfad9aae-c985-469f-9412-8c038d695fe2" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에 있습니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e25d17be-aa97-4e47-8441-1aa7f2b35251" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-4e7675c5-685e-4a2f-b818-3688ca0c8954" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f050e6ea-b5d1-4021-ba55-0f103c20dcea" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-991d5804-c96b-4518-9698-f2c6cca6648e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">C. 기술 차별점 2: 'Egress Fee 제로'라는 비즈니스 모델 혁신</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-49c90b8f-732b-4bab-bd09-4f37f5aff7b9" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-43c64c3f-243c-4663-8336-396a1d1ea003" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-35f5aa61-558f-4e5c-8a67-b73d89174219" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-63c96b0e-36d7-4715-9c23-e4a4c7c26dbe" style="color:#1b1c1d;">어닝 콜 Q&amp;A에서 가장 중요한 차별점으로 'Egress Fee'(데이터 전송 수수료)가 언급되었습니다[User Query].</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e778ee3e-dc57-4213-a2ac-3deaad11459a" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a3981b3f-36c1-419c-b595-1d655a313632" style="color:#1b1c1d;">하이퍼스케일러의 '세금': AWS, GCP 등은 자사 클라우드에 데이터를 저장(Ingress)할 때는 저렴하지만, 데이터를 외부로 빼낼 때(Egress) 막대한 요금을 부과합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6ef9146e-d686-496d-a209-b1d7b7dbc73e" style="color:#444746;">59</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f67eb2fe-e38f-41a6-b702-bb9fb18e13b8" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이는 고객의 데이터를 자사 생태계에 '독점'[User Query] 또는 'Lock-in'하기 위한 전략적 '세금'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d6eb44fa-7f9b-44b7-8bb2-f29cb6c9ce89" style="color:#444746;">26</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c147b960-4907-4f86-af15-93c7ab4372a4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5540540e-4415-41ec-8c88-27fa1d5c29ce" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e1de48ac-8a14-4619-baca-a5ebd9679b95" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare의 '해방':</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3c2274a6-526c-4c83-aee7-e64b3993585e" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-63be2277-8775-436f-b124-fa67bbd4886c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">R2 Storage: Cloudflare의 R2</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-922394fa-c61b-4060-9c69-6f8c3071961a" style="color:#444746;">61</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-bf8eb56d-ca3f-4f69-be2c-6f6e3ffb030f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">는 AWS S3와 완벽히 호환</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a74f51e4-f4c6-4527-81b0-a7faf8062ed3" style="color:#444746;">62</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9911d27e-2671-4e71-bb7d-070568849e6a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">되지만, Egress Fee가 0원입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0626c4ce-039a-4041-9027-7e30a3487d62" style="color:#444746;">63</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-06eb1db4-1349-4a8b-87c0-8eaf98444946" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-28fad2e4-49ac-497d-b725-28aaca0ec771" style="color:#1b1c1d;">전략적 함의: AI 기업은 수 페타바이트(PB)의 학습 데이터를 R2에 저장</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-646036fb-de31-46c8-8de1-a310303f5f52" style="color:#444746;">62</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a43517c4-25ea-450c-97c0-cd253d31063a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">할 수 있습니다. 그리고 이 데이터를 AWS, GCP, OCI, 또는 Cloudflare Workers 등 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-e694986a-1c9f-477f-8925-7e56f0671a45" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>어떤 컴퓨팅 플랫폼</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f8b5d6f7-ca3b-447b-9fc1-b41bf2b50440" style="color:#1b1c1d;">으로든 Egress Fee 없이 자유롭게 이동하여 AI 모델을 학습시키거나 추론할 수 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-90f3d77b-5653-48ad-b251-3e31b4e6f58a" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4174f1c0-9587-48d7-a4e1-757df9f88610" style="color:#1b1c1d;">결론: R2는 하이퍼스케일러의 가장 강력한 비즈니스 모델(Lock-in)을 정면으로 파괴합니다. 이는 고객에게 '데이터 유연성'[User Query]을 제공하며, Cloudflare를 멀티 클라우드 시대의 필수적인 중립적 스토리지 허브로 만듭니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c71465fa-02b1-4b5f-abdd-bb160ae0aca7" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-db6235c0-ef3c-4473-ab29-adb349c26705" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare의 세 가지 핵심 전략(PoF, R2, Workers AI)은 완벽하게 맞물려 돌아갑니다. 한 Fortune 100 기업</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-da1c5667-e6b8-44b3-852e-03eca7e6cd74" style="color:#444746;">27</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b81d99de-16be-4a4e-ab1a-d0cdec40c439" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이 $20M 규모의 'Pool of Funds' 계약을 체결하면, 이 기업은 하이퍼스케일러의 Egress Fee를 피하기 위해 자사의 모든 AI/ML 학습 데이터를 **'R2'(Egress Fee 제로)**로 이전합니다. 그리고 이 기업의 개발자들은 이제 이 R2 데이터를 기반으로 PoF 예산을 사용해 **'Workers AI'(엣지 추론)**에서 애플리케이션을 구축합니다. 이 세 가지 요소가 결합되어 Cloudflare의 플랫폼에 대한 강력한 'Lock-in'을 생성하며, 이는 하이퍼스케일러의 '데이터 독점'이 아닌 '플랫폼 가치'에 기반한 긍정적인 Lock-in입니다.</span></p><!-- } SE-TEXT -->
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div> <div class="se-component se-horizontalLine se-l-line1" id="SE-ae7a865b-6a94-48b2-8cc8-07e25c76a3e3">
<div class="se-component-content">
<div class="se-section se-section-horizontalLine se-l-line1 se-section-align-">
<div class="se-module se-module-horizontalLine">
<hr class="se-hr"/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div> <div class="se-component se-text se-l-default" id="SE-41f69eec-824f-4bd5-ae0e-cc4f8b006053">
<div class="se-component-content">
<div class="se-section se-section-text se-l-default">
<div class="se-module se-module-text">
<!-- SE-TEXT { --><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8ed81311-4f4d-47b0-b432-1c41d1768ee4" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-fef0dd9b-1221-486e-9a75-e0cc43d6c300" style="color:#1b1c1d;">VI. 멀티 클라우드 시대의 "접착제": 파트너십 및 경쟁 구도</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d3d967d2-5f67-4b06-8838-6965741ad945" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-726dfd0e-36bc-4eb7-9ce1-4770d3c5addd" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c5cc01c8-734d-4d4d-96a2-c67d800f7691" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ea866e7a-f82c-4794-954b-91667d4323f3" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare의 전략은 모든 것을 대체하는 것이 아니라, 기존 클라우드들을 '연결'하고 '보호'하며 '가속'하는 중립적 '접착제'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5cc6dcc3-f398-4339-b7a5-90e1332275ab" style="color:#444746;">26</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-54c43dc6-01ec-4745-80fc-a7d4eb4632f7" style="color:#1b1c1d;">가 되는 것입니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-44d95244-e5cc-429f-84c5-4832282d8190" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-0255dd2c-130c-4ae4-ba69-fae99ddefe4d" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5bfae0e7-39d0-4ee6-9b0e-579e96915c59" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-52568548-85c5-4bd3-93b4-e4ef86090554" style="color:#1b1c1d;">A. OCI(오라클 클라우드) 파트너십의 전략적 의미</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-dcbc6d81-a86c-417c-a091-6a868a45a794" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-3b2aecca-76aa-4491-9e9d-d74d87e6033d" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-86e9f85a-f632-46c4-afad-c44115971057" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d4638559-4b39-42ce-ae46-f5fe6c0962ee" style="color:#1b1c1d;">어닝 콜 Q&amp;A에서 OCI가 특별히 언급된[User Query] 것은 우연이 아닙니다.</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d307278e-785b-44c3-a85b-e2e0f808986c" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-24d58eae-6b42-4bac-b5f5-d548442ff178" style="color:#1b1c1d;">배경: OCI와 Cloudflare는 2021년부터 'Bandwidth Alliance'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-31f2e3f2-dcee-4c13-a976-c005c4937f45" style="color:#444746;">65</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-98e374e3-1f93-442b-81f4-1782ac332be1" style="color:#1b1c1d;">라는 이름으로 파트너십을 맺고 있습니다. 이 동맹의 핵심은 양사 간의 데이터 전송 수수료(Egress Fee)를 '제로(0)'로 만드는 것입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-762d3c77-d35e-42be-baf6-d6554c22d27a" style="color:#444746;">66</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-2f7a4616-b92c-42a7-ae5c-470d48fac1f2" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f26e5b4d-ee37-4fad-b11e-f908fca9135a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">전략적 시너지: OCI는 AWS/GCP 대비 저렴하고 강력한 베어메탈/GPU 컴퓨팅 성능</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e723ac74-12cc-46c5-a1d9-81bb9c3fd09b" style="color:#444746;">67</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6ea2e68d-7507-4113-9e4a-407e7497cb63" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 제공하고자 합니다. Cloudflare는 Egress Fee를 없애 멀티 클라우드를 활성화하고자 합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d78952a4-494b-4639-8ad0-11d393edd200" style="color:#444746;">68</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f9a565a1-c9a5-4254-8b8b-3875e752e050" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-34b960c6-ff3b-4208-8e27-dec46ec7a370" style="color:#1b1c1d;">시나리오: 고객은 컴퓨팅 집약적인 워크로드는 OCI에서 실행하고, 데이터 저장은 R2에서 하며, 이 모든 트래픽을 Cloudflare의 보안 및 엣지(CDN/WAF/SASE)를 통해 전송합니다. 이 과정에서 값비싼 Egress Fee가 발생하지 않습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e1fb3a22-e8e1-4dd4-8b4a-1e51274a797b" style="color:#444746;">69</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ddc5ae9a-8721-4088-81a7-7506b1ac80f8" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-86318201-45c8-491b-a929-d6c30fa592f0" style="color:#1b1c1d;">결론: 이 파트너십은 AWS의 'Egress Fee 세금'을 우회하는 강력한 '반-AWS' 동맹입니다. 어닝 콜에서 "멀티클라우드가 증가할수록 당사의 인프라를 요구하게 됨"[User Query]이라는 발언은 OCI와 같은 파트너십을 통해 하이퍼스케일러들 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-9ac7a407-cdb8-456b-89ba-690a6f5c20b7" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>사이</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b4cda5d4-f492-466f-9f4c-c09d5e421979" style="color:#1b1c1d;">의 중립적 인프라가 되겠다는 전략을 의미합니다. 최근에는 AI 워크로드 보안 강화</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b37d9a9d-8156-481f-b8f4-ebdf8d9c82a8" style="color:#444746;">70</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3fb098f0-6cfe-4477-a480-1379fd1dd0a3" style="color:#1b1c1d;">로 협력이 확대되고 있습니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-36d356a6-6f22-4a0d-a5a5-a152a148dfa6" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-93d9c799-0366-4460-bae2-f0e1ed7f77cc" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8a1e171f-f7bf-4618-a58e-846d946945f3" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7e3432cc-8dc8-4a5c-8759-e7848af1047d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">B. 경쟁 구도: SASE &amp; Zero Trust</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0a97feb3-6c7c-4fc3-bd6f-0c76b904fa0e" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-803d08b5-1e98-4572-836d-fe3ddfe2bcd5" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-12c586f4-c9b2-457e-8b5f-18c1b357a7f0" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9088958e-f6c5-4b4e-b824-62e27216ceeb" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare One(SASE)은 엔터프라이즈 시장에서 가장 치열한 격전지입니다.</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-27864463-d0ee-4925-b266-001fec0b7d83" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ae1ea135-8e63-4c20-9db4-e50d2affcdb3" style="color:#1b1c1d;">vs. Zscaler (ZS): Zscaler는 SASE 시장의 선두주자입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9b640124-d892-40c6-b5b8-54ab0fd438e1" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e062ac7e-35ba-44ab-8d01-6e1a9ed54e71" style="color:#1b1c1d;">G2 리뷰 비교</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3d8c6f42-5e4d-4efc-bcf4-5557d5fee9d8" style="color:#444746;">44</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4d2f2c98-5c0b-4140-8a95-ded8ab1542d0" style="color:#1b1c1d;">: 사용자들은 Cloudflare의 '사용 편의성'(9.2 vs 8.9)과 '지원 품질'(9.2 vs 8.9)을 더 높게 평가합니다. 반면 Zscaler는 'DLP'(9.3 vs 9.0)와 '애플리케이션 보안'(9.4 vs 8.9)에서 소폭 우세합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c1883382-e777-4cf6-b0c7-ad5aa9b34975" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f727c4b1-a77f-4edb-aef1-e4370a649259" style="color:#1b1c1d;">아키텍처</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ef696579-2941-419f-9ffd-9c46b4e44d56" style="color:#444746;">48</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a4a8dede-e895-4ea1-8776-11112b7d7664" style="color:#1b1c1d;">: Cloudflare는 단일 플랫폼/데몬으로 실행되지만, Zscaler는 ZIA와 ZPA를 분리 운영하고 VM 인프라를 요구하는 등 아키텍처가 더 복잡할 수 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ea19473b-8f8d-4713-a5b5-dc9d92a75888" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-daa1f7fa-f50e-468b-9071-299a98458b89" style="color:#1b1c1d;">vs. Palo Alto Networks (PANW) &amp; Fortinet (FTNT): 이들은 전통적인 방화벽 하드웨어 강자에서 클라우드로 전환 중입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-46bc97ae-f574-411b-ad15-69feb1bd9c98" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-102a3184-1028-4d87-b045-038342c74384" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Gartner 리뷰 비교</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-47416b1c-55f1-4c3b-9ce3-9c7afd152e64" style="color:#444746;">45</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5d0c7c78-fbec-480f-9b7e-88a067a94f7f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">: 이들 모두 SASE, SSE, ZTNA 시장에서 Cloudflare와 직접 경쟁합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-60e2a9a0-d669-4441-89fe-db35d55e8d25" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e92853ca-e7e5-4dec-a40b-446b64f9db93" style="color:#1b1c1d;">G2 리뷰 비교</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c0df99dd-17cd-4baf-bbf2-8ccba0970648" style="color:#444746;">75</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8c5b5da2-8f1e-4004-98c9-f7a43496f1d3" style="color:#1b1c1d;">: PANW와 FTNT는 '방화벽 기능'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c13b75c1-96b9-4cb7-a0d5-b236c41ab90f" style="color:#444746;">75</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-049109bb-fc50-4ac1-a0f0-3435d2773126" style="color:#1b1c1d;">과 같은 전통적 보안 기능에서 강력한 평가를 받습니다. 하지만 Cloudflare는 '설치 용이성'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b5d573b7-2766-4952-91d2-756a746d94fd" style="color:#444746;">76</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c45d280f-b374-4307-a741-7a3fd1aa590c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">과 '동적 확장성'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7c4f35d5-ddc0-4eaa-b8db-821f5fc6e3d1" style="color:#444746;">76</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e3476d14-270c-4626-a9a2-3baf9b9836ab" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 등 클라우드 네이티브 환경에서 더 높은 평가를 받습니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-7b516c69-52d7-47c9-9e47-31438805a487" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-863889f5-dd11-44bd-99ac-ea145167cb7b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">SASE 시장의 트렌드는 '단일 벤더 SASE'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-05ec09f7-faa9-44c5-bf21-8e7e3ac6f788" style="color:#444746;">78</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-930500e5-6304-44f1-aecb-21857542c393" style="color:#1b1c1d;">로 수렴하고 있습니다. 경쟁사들이 인수를 통해 여러 기술을 '꿰매어' SASE를 제공하는 반면</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-df672819-5c3d-40f6-81a1-74e88a3c0f51" style="color:#444746;">42</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2f5bce7f-36d5-49e7-a084-67abc74e082d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, Cloudflare의 경쟁 우위는 자사의 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-b64ab368-873b-43d4-8b46-973c8f9496be" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>단일</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d966e50c-09ac-463c-aaec-f702cc33fb36" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 글로벌 네트워크</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c2f17ced-3e9d-4c2f-b09f-362da4c741d1" style="color:#444746;">3</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-68e6b42d-94b5-401e-929e-ee2c78148f8c" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 위에서 CDN, SASE, Workers AI를 모두 유기적으로 실행한다는 아키텍처의 단순성, 성능, 통합 관리</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-65ff8adf-7be0-451e-bd26-eba0c871321b" style="color:#444746;">73</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e411deab-9c46-4921-b1f2-4f660ebcd754" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에 있습니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-781738a4-a93d-4c07-bab7-be1581cbb998" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-627ffcfb-a168-4e0f-a617-6d8e141ce1d9" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d1250dee-fa20-4d93-9f68-7a81b9383339" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-11bd4d78-e960-41f2-ac91-256fb63fd9b1" style="color:#1b1c1d;">C. 경쟁 구도: 하이퍼스케일러 (AWS, GCP, Azure)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-de6b6cdd-436d-407f-a57b-0e805611a3da" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-412acd84-621a-4e52-8958-b42bcef46f64" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1e2b4f77-7a98-44d3-a700-6895da7315c3" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-84e91cdd-6b20-491a-b42e-b5da70a6d395" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare는 하이퍼스케일러와 'Co-opetition'(협력적 경쟁) 관계</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-94c206ff-1b04-4023-a57a-4544eaea0f31" style="color:#444746;">26</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-78d3f071-61b7-41bf-aa7c-f84795d90401" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에 있습니다. AWS의 S3를 대체(R2)하고 CloudFront(CDN)와 경쟁</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e7ef087b-2656-4bfc-ac0e-7b2aa866492c" style="color:#444746;">79</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-674162fe-e4e4-4b09-862d-c3b9478b4eae" style="color:#1b1c1d;">하지만, 동시에 AWS/GCP/Azure에서 실행되는 애플리케이션을 보호하고 가속화합니다. 핵심 공략 지점은 하이퍼스케일러의 'Egress Fee 세금'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c8a21ffc-0ffd-4e0e-aa4c-cee7db6c2232" style="color:#444746;">59</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a408bd3e-2ae7-489e-879a-765cfdf5505b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이며, Cloudflare는 고객이 특정 하이퍼스케일러에 종속되지 않고 자유롭게 클라우드를 선택할 수 있도록 돕는 '멀티 클라우드 네비게이터' 역할을 수행합니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-2fec14fa-0f02-4cac-9412-dee8b28bfb72" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a6609f90-660b-45da-ad14-dab531495d70" style="color:#1b1c1d;">표 3: SASE/Zero Trust 기능 비교 (Cloudflare vs. Zscaler vs. Palo Alto Networks)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-896aeb8e-8fa9-44a6-9329-099ec6eaf0ff" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-2c5f3cf9-d275-4a59-8f06-885e8d99db20" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><!-- } SE-TEXT -->
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div> <div class="se-component se-table se-l-default" id="SE-a1935027-d81e-4d3b-af55-2cb78491abac">
<div class="se-component-content">
<div class="se-section se-section-table se-l-default se-section-align-left" style="width: 100.0%;">
<div class="se-table-container">
<table class="se-table-content" style="border:none;border-collapse:collapse;">
<tbody><tr class="se-tr"><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-76768824-da1d-4ce8-99f5-5cd9c0ab81b8" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1ac520e2-e0b2-49e9-a1f4-d427e3eb3a1e" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">기능/특징</span></p></div>
</td><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-99c13a83-573e-495b-8876-ff99cdcc9cdb" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d0d9538c-4189-4e9e-a2a1-22f0ded6605e" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">Cloudflare (Cloudflare One)</span></p></div>
</td><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-231864d3-53b8-4e8c-9ff2-a4135cdddbb0" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ef6d3a7c-f932-4350-98ed-c3a38327ef88" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">Zscaler (ZIA/ZPA)</span></p></div>
</td><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b85d10c0-1cf6-4bfa-a073-7d14f665a84b" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-eb579603-5e57-4bc5-81dd-465a257fd88b" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">Palo Alto Networks (Prisma SASE)</span></p></div>
</td></tr><tr class="se-tr"><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-033540fa-a337-49c0-bac2-7f0699a629a0" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0668b9fa-20d5-43d8-9370-160a8f0049fd" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">플랫폼 아키텍처</span></p></div>
</td><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-104f5e55-03ff-489c-a4b1-9ff63a850cca" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9d342521-c8b1-49c0-8eff-f52cbfb470b5" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">단일 통합 네트워크. 모든 서비스가 모든 엣지 노드에서 실행. </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-88bee4a1-174b-48c4-ac3f-627cb887439a" style="color:#444746;background-color:#f8fafd;">48</span></p></div>
</td><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e514cd92-da6d-4e93-963b-3343ac94bb64" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-32d1fdcb-6b8f-4fba-9478-99acb469af17" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">분리된 서비스 게이트웨이 (ZIA/ZPA). VM 인프라 필요. </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-89182775-5ed9-4609-b54d-15ab01f8e7f7" style="color:#444746;background-color:#f8fafd;">48</span></p></div>
</td><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a01959ba-18b0-4422-8a80-8575f78d45e4" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f6510651-425a-4038-a477-17a6e157cad9" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">인수 기반 통합 (CloudGenix, Aporeto 등). Panorama로 중앙 관리. </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b49baa89-2744-4022-835f-b47ac3f489b0" style="color:#444746;background-color:#f8fafd;">76</span></p></div>
</td></tr><tr class="se-tr"><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-eed3dfed-34d6-4e95-b3be-e54b1a533a6d" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-44d2cb57-52b8-4e4c-95f6-109bd0e665da" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">핵심 기능 (G2평가)</span></p></div>
</td><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">
<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9df39692-4a8a-426e-b966-1b65bbb1ce10" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-da6ed0f1-8464-4810-a1f5-3fb341407087" style="color:#1b1c1d;background-color:#f8fafd;">사용 편의성(9.2/10), 지원 품질(9.2/10). </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-64d22349-f922-45fd-9284-e76d71f99adf" style="color:#444746;background-color:#f8fafd;">44</span></p></div>
</td><td class="se-cell" colspan="1" rowspan="1" style="width: 25.0%; height: 40.0px; border:1px solid #000000; background-color: #f8fafd;">

<!-- SE-TEXT { --><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8c501501-b440-4d16-a79b-2bd14d08d9da" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-97cf2579-1a4f-47f7-9e6f-1ccc8e78f52e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">VII. 리스크 분석 및 약세론 (Bear Case)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-7a2fec26-d2ef-4188-85d0-2ac22a91d160" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-dbdcf1e2-d2c0-490c-9805-18bf60e720a8" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-04421b15-077f-4012-a864-c7dcf484ebc5" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-555c193f-b633-48db-8986-0b8796e04769" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare의 강력한 성장 스토리에도 불구하고, 투자자는 다음과 같은 심각한 리스크 요인을 반드시 인지해야 합니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-bc5719ab-5e71-4a6c-9897-41b4f3961837" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-b2a253fe-36b3-4b34-a427-61571863e495" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-89142d0e-5b99-4bd8-b7ee-cdf536afdab0" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4b0fc081-07bc-4403-8c86-61a1e774c2e4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">A. 최대 리스크: 높은 밸류에이션 (Valuation)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-6afe865b-722d-45be-aacc-3d793c0fea65" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-fb6315ef-6f9a-4acf-85a0-f1fe7d342d1e" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-724768c7-cfd7-4365-8526-7fafe3fbf49a" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-cc9a5bb9-f470-4771-a2a1-88c477ea2360" style="color:#1b1c1d;">주가 수준: Cloudflare의 주가는 3분기 실적 발표 후 급등하며</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-60b17b76-9626-48cc-804c-f258328eb797" style="color:#444746;">1</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ace0b638-9200-4207-bbbc-efc012ca7219" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 사상 최고가에 근접했습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-302d7aed-98f0-4280-8ae0-45374ec534ed" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-05765e7b-4597-45e3-a9c2-1f1f3c4bded4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">고평가 지표:</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c8fb0cdb-5b61-4688-9877-f986b64c3be5" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-04196be1-ae93-4f53-b8e8-ef4a054f81f6" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>DCF 분석:</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-258e5015-83d8-479e-bcdc-cf6e5cfaeeaa" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 다수의 DCF 모델 분석 결과, 현재 주가는 내재가치 대비 157%에서 197%까지 "심각하게 고평가"(Significantly overvalued)되어 있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1d0828e7-b600-48a0-a650-6a30a20f02f3" style="color:#444746;">81</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-038a4c77-0279-4a18-8bca-09e195d2ea6a" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-bc6b4dc4-e75c-483e-8493-5fb4890171e4" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>P/S 비율:</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b5fdd92b-b800-4e10-9d99-3bdf463fd726" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> P/S(주가매출비율)는 44x 수준으로, 업계 평균이나 내재가치(약 18.5x) 대비 2배 이상 높게 거래되고 있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-007a148f-1698-4616-a923-57a792f28e5e" style="color:#444746;">82</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e6893c39-7d15-4318-b0b7-33bf31fc558f" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-70882a08-778a-4d05-89ab-d1f39d2d4da9" style="color:#1b1c1d;">의미: 현재 주가는 AI 전략의 완벽한 실행과 향후 몇 년간의 고성장을 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-99188b04-89d7-4a39-b6f6-8147bab2acf0" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>이미 모두 반영</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a654e218-bcb7-46fe-86dc-175d78de52f1" style="color:#1b1c1d;">하고 있습니다. 만약 AI 매출(현재 '미미함'[User Query])이 기대만큼 빠르게 가시화되지 않거나, 단 한 분기라도 RPO 성장이 둔화되는 조짐을 보인다면, 주가는 밸류에이션 부담으로 인해 급격한 조정을 받을 수 있습니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-7f466e4b-51d6-4a63-96b3-07cb9c585820" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-2608d4f3-b6f9-45cd-9b8c-ce3ab8eb5b2a" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-39eb9c87-cd56-4286-83bf-9429311f7fb3" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-08f1c48a-88fe-42fe-8062-f8e5c4ea5151" style="color:#1b1c1d;">B. 실행 리스크: 핵심 리더십 공백 (CJ Desai의 이탈)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1fd2bf08-e83f-4360-9e87-adb93b7acfb0" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-f390c569-3554-4fb4-9321-0a4c86b0b71f" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ce2fd1dc-91bc-4689-9cd1-1b2f6c49d466" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ed4507b9-8227-456d-8346-3199e268d04b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">사건: Cloudflare는 Q3 실적 발표(10월 30일)</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-23c2482a-c3ef-4b57-b620-0ca0b3a22287" style="color:#444746;">6</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-395572d4-6d51-42d4-95f7-78e950856dd7" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 직후, 제품 및 엔지니어링 사장(President of Product &amp; Engineering)인 CJ Desai가 11월 7일부로 사임하고 MongoDB (MDB)의 CEO로 이직</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c28db14c-a5b4-496a-9810-f4f524fe2cd5" style="color:#444746;">83</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-006221df-99e9-4e74-9a23-796b31f34cc4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">한다고 발표했습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-65074469-3018-44a2-9aa7-529d0de9d2c6" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-06dedf20-9ded-410f-91dc-d48bc6d2f57a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">심각성: 이는 사소한 인사이동이 아닙니다.</span></p></li></ul><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9ca7a18e-f557-4a80-879c-6ce4076d7f40" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a531c59a-8569-4796-919c-d073b2abb4fb" style="color:#1b1c1d;">초단기 재임: CJ Desai는 ServiceNow의 COO로서 회사를 $1.5B에서 $10B 규모로 성장시킨</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-69e61c4f-6ba8-4cc9-8616-6bb5fa6a1069" style="color:#444746;">87</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-532e398b-b398-4165-93dd-a8dfda99b740" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 스타 경영자였습니다. 그는 Cloudflare의 "$5B 매출 목표 달성"이라는 명확한 임무를 받고 2024년 10월 10일에 합류했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-01c40126-f3cf-4c41-a835-e3ca8da59592" style="color:#444746;">87</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f40785d4-7926-4f56-be24-0a41a8117fa7" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-685b980a-1dac-417d-9926-d4384f80f54f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">단 13개월 만의 이탈: 그의 재임 기간은 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-d9ae1d65-40ea-4aca-b45b-2603d45ad0f7" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>고작 13개월</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-87eecd77-7782-4544-ad9c-e5ae645f2ef5" style="color:#1b1c1d;">입니다. Cloudflare의 핵심인 제품과 엔지니어링 조직 전체를 총괄하던 수장의 갑작스러운 공백은 엔터프라이즈 GTM 및 AI 제품 로드맵 실행에 심각한 차질을 초래할 수 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a443de3d-d9a4-4ae6-81ca-32fb673d809a" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-aa91e558-7062-4487-af06-11778acafc34" style="color:#1b1c1d;">시기: 어닝 콜에서 RPO 가속화와 엔터프라이즈 영업의 성공을 자축한 직후에</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1062e08c-6546-47b9-9abf-94f2783b4e60" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-87ebd302-363e-41b3-9223-a664b4414d85" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 그 전략을 실행해야 할 핵심 임원이 회사를 떠난다는 것은 심각한 '레드 플래그'입니다.</span></p></li></ol><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3d71a97b-50c2-4120-8b98-864900e57f2c" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-78fd7527-4862-48c7-aa54-59525c30bc1f" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-434c88d0-e958-41c4-9f43-53b07a1e7c69" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e8b86f36-7261-467f-8a18-dba087694998" style="color:#1b1c1d;">C. 재무 리스크: AI 투자의 이익률 압박</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ff31a078-915f-4aed-adcb-d692e80434e5" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-cbde6158-451f-4be6-9a25-6200ebdddc98" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3469b424-7fde-4017-b072-9e4f51b3efa7" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-188090ce-810c-4621-8854-8823b43778e8" style="color:#1b1c1d;">딜레마: Cloudflare는 AI 추론 시장을 선점하기 위해 값비싼 GPU(H100 등) 투자를 늘리고 있습니다[User Query].</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-fa1b2e9a-67f1-4bce-aa7f-a4e3ad3fc88d" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-da249201-0bb1-4770-84be-6b0efa81a371" style="color:#1b1c1d;">결과: 이 투자는 즉각적으로 총이익률(Gross Margin)을 압박</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-95d1b502-d522-4b03-be6e-28853835263b" style="color:#444746;">15</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9b9cd804-1d14-4554-b978-40b74fc19b87" style="color:#1b1c1d;">하고 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-868a88fd-d110-4474-a7aa-6d574726beee" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-db5ce4f9-baea-41ba-a7e4-bc4d7f5c8fcc" style="color:#1b1c1d;">리스크: 현재 AI 추론 매출은 "미미한 수준"[User Query]입니다. 즉, AI는 현재 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-4de6922d-722d-49f3-a3e9-7291b5249226" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>비용</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-44329ca1-13b1-4677-b662-78aaf9078f60" style="color:#1b1c1d;">만 발생시키고 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a518279e-7798-4e97-b30a-2455781b0057" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>수익</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f9464715-60e2-4810-880a-b29529358a06" style="color:#1b1c1d;">은 거의 없는 상태입니다. 만약 AI Gateway를 통한 업셀링 전략이 실패하고 Workers AI의 상업화가 늦어진다면, 회사는 'AI 투자는 증가, 수익은 정체'라는 최악의 시나리오에 직면할 수 있으며, 이는 수익성 악화로 직결됩니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-7b318910-b383-4a0e-bfb0-3c3c87b7eaf1" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-b0a7a278-e051-4fea-bb8d-3767dc067bce" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-32716e8c-a1c4-4554-afe7-97e71ae1f36f" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3b211072-5c17-4a63-9177-0a4195a1df81" style="color:#1b1c1d;">D. 경쟁 리스크: 하이퍼스케일러의 반격</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e780fbdc-5946-4352-91f6-13bebc22f23a" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-ccd86056-b71f-43f5-a0da-321f2e322e45" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d654c84c-6a51-48cb-ab08-101c32e36f85" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6fc71684-789e-4f7b-afea-095f4ac13ce7" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Egress Fee의 미래: Cloudflare의 핵심 무기(Egress Fee 제로)는 하이퍼스케일러가 이 '세금'을 인하할 경우 그 위력이 약화될 수 있습니다. </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1c0acf7d-0cba-49d5-bb68-5834766f686d" style="color:#444746;">89</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-db5f5d11-f19c-45c8-98bd-7d184be275b2" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-626bd395-5543-4486-b62b-e229f23251ec" style="color:#1b1c1d;">엣지 경쟁: AWS(CloudFront), GCP, Azure 역시 자신들의 엣지(Edge) 역량을 강화하고 있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0b5b2343-5627-4e10-bc68-6d84f9dc174f" style="color:#444746;">89</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-157f269a-210c-4b9c-9429-7ed9a857750b" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 만약 이들이 Cloudflare의 R2와 유사한 'Egress Fee 제로' 스토리지나 더 강력한 엣지 컴퓨팅을 제공하기 시작한다면, Cloudflare의 차별점은 빠르게 사라질 수 있습니다.</span></p></li></ul><!-- } SE-TEXT -->
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div> <div class="se-component se-horizontalLine se-l-line1" id="SE-50f965d0-3b81-460b-baf1-aa9a0cf38989">

</div> <div class="se-component se-text se-l-default" id="SE-0c6d8b84-7e71-4052-b6a9-c76713f05d24">
<div class="se-component-content">
<div class="se-section se-section-text se-l-default">
<div class="se-module se-module-text">
<!-- SE-TEXT { --><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-47596514-b20b-489a-b38b-0df741fd27c1" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c11d14ff-57d6-46cd-be99-16e6ebdc83d9" style="color:#1b1c1d;">VIII. 결론 및 투자 전략</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-50782add-3c3c-49d9-b3b6-4a3485074f6a" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-6cf5a125-0a6e-4b90-8cb1-273ba88c1cb9" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c0c6a0ab-627a-48ed-aaef-52c59cea61fe" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9c4bc290-4a97-4468-aa1d-fa8b7afb8894" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Cloudflare는 Q3 FY25 실적을 통해 엔터프라이즈 GTM 전략과 플랫폼 통합(SASE, Workers)이 성공적으로 작동하고 있음을 RPO 43% 성장이라는 명확한 숫자로 증명했습니다.</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1ba1c47e-888a-4f2f-90e0-2aa1f7002919" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4d9f1c6d-feab-46f4-87c3-e0222eb68fb6" style="color:#1b1c1d;">강세론(Bull Case) 요약: Cloudflare는 CDN(Act 1), SASE(Act 2), 엣지 AI(Act 3)라는 세 개의 거대 시장을 '단일 엣지 네트워크'로 통합하는 유일한 기업입니다. 하이퍼스케일러의 Egress Fee '세금'을 무력화하는 R2 스토리지</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c59a60fb-809b-42ff-b010-6295130852f6" style="color:#444746;">61</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-bc1f0425-4b66-4099-bb44-80d4a5c5970f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">와, GPU를 효율적으로 공유하는 'Omni'/'Infire'</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4372ead7-1861-4b98-b1f8-8ed110aecf5e" style="color:#444746;">57</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6f5a0476-e550-43fe-bd63-de7fefbf6d9c" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 기술은 모방하기 어려운 강력한 해자입니다. OCI와 같은 전략적 파트너십</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8a8ccab0-d409-40f9-968a-002d120ad552" style="color:#444746;">66</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e4322423-c9b9-40fd-a203-b9196ba8b728" style="color:#1b1c1d;">은 멀티 클라우드 시대의 '스위스'라는 중립적 지위를 강화합니다. Q3 실적은 이 모든 전략이 $5B 목표</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-35da5d0c-a3b2-4c0d-b53c-633f2b7e6b81" style="color:#444746;">8</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-af53a2d8-c73c-427d-96e7-bde4f8f05d13" style="color:#1b1c1d;">를 향해 가속화되고 있음을 보여줍니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5d659906-b8ee-4ae2-bc51-3c2574cf0ae3" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1ad0da98-8c07-4538-84b2-6d3591d98c20" style="color:#1b1c1d;">약세론(Bear Case) 요약: 밸류에이션</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-edd5d608-5aa8-4b5b-81ad-a0553939e3be" style="color:#444746;">82</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ba6ca89f-f477-45a2-95d8-d002a2c8ba8f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">은 이미 완벽한 미래를 가정하고 있어, 작은 실수에도 주가는 치명적일 수 있습니다. AI 전략은 아직 '비용'(GM 압박)</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-54ef5641-bb19-42f7-b953-1fe72fe65241" style="color:#444746;">15</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-90098a0c-1164-42f5-92c0-9543171b1f2c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이 '수익'(매출 미미)[User Query]을 압도합니다. 무엇보다 엔터프라이즈 확장을 진두지휘해야 할 CJ Desai의 13개월 만의 이탈</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-96e8d410-8cc3-4cf2-9124-f8bff10c59f1" style="color:#444746;">86</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3a46fa7e-6224-4f71-ab26-a5f2ad748d50" style="color:#1b1c1d;">은 이 완벽한 시나리오에 심각한 '실행 리스크'를 더합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-280b5a41-f460-42c4-831e-2b6b8c769a51" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-36ebef4e-4757-4971-837c-8e9112ecc27c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">최종 투자 의견: Cloudflare는 장기적인 구조적 성장 스토리를 보유한 'Game Changer'입니다. Q3 실적은 이들의 전략적 방향성이 옳았음을 입증했습니다. 밸류에이션은 매우 부담스러운 수준이나, RPO 가속화로 확인된 성장 모멘텀은 강력합니다. CJ Desai의 이탈은 단기적으로 가장 우려되는 리스크이며, 후임자 임명과 제품 로드맵의 연속성을 면밀히 모니터링해야 합니다. 하지만 Cloudflare의 기술적 해자와 거대한 TAM을 고려할 때, 밸류에이션 리스크를 감수할 수 있는 장기 투자자에게는 여전히 매력적인 핵심 보유 자산입니다. AI 매출의 가시화가 시작되는 시점에 주가는 추가적인 재평가(re-rating)가 가능할 것입니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-cf2d64a1-416f-4d8d-8538-2667e7201d00" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs16 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a8110d8b-edd4-41ec-a165-8754906138bc" style="color:#000000;">참고 자료</span></p><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-87160164-acc1-458e-8260-6648c61c8df5" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs16 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e547057c-9ff8-4ff6-9ee0-bce067009f35" style="color:#000000;">Cloudflare Stock Surges Almost 14% As Q3 Results Crush Estimates, 11월 9, 2025에 액세스, </span><span class="se-fs-fs16 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0ea4b387-6c72-4168-b099-11d67e304ba1" style="color:#0000ee;"><a class="se-link __se_link" target="_blank"><u>https://www.tikr.com/blog/cloudflare-stock-surges-almost-14-as-q3-results-crush-estimates</u></a></span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5350210c-6009-4edd-886d-2c4214ac4b42" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs16 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset"
